vibecomfy: model-distribution v1 — extract HuggingFace URLs from workflow JSONs, populate READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"], add a small fetch CLI and doctor integration. Don't build a bundle/manifest format yet.

Source repo: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy

# Problem
Today vibecomfy has a half-built model-distribution story:
- Upstream Comfy-Org workflow JSONs in `workflow_corpus/official/*.json` already carry inline `"url": "https://huggingface.co/..."` fields per model-loader node (see workflow_corpus/official/video/wan_t2v.json:38, ltx2_3_i2v.json:699, flux2_klein_4b_t2i.json:1082, etc.) plus markdown notes in human-readable form.
- The materializer in `scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py` builds `ready_templates/*/*.py` from those JSONs but never extracts the URLs — every scratchpad has `READY_REQUIREMENTS = {"models": [], "custom_nodes": [...]}` with `models` empty.
- There is no downloader. `vibecomfy doctor` checks runtime readiness but doesn't check model presence.
- The de-facto convention is HuggingFace via the upstream Comfy-Org URL fields, but nothing in vibecomfy reads or enforces it.

# Goal
Close the loop in five mechanical steps:
1. Materializer extracts URLs from workflow JSON and populates `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]` with structured entries.
2. A small `vibecomfy.fetch` module downloads missing models from URLs to the right ComfyUI subdir.
3. `vibecomfy fetch <workflow>` CLI command resolves a workflow's model list and downloads the missing ones.
4. `vibecomfy doctor <workflow>` reports missing models with their URLs.
5. Tests for extraction, fetch (mocked httpx), and doctor.

The bundle/manifest format and SHA pinning are explicitly OUT of scope — they're a separate plan.

# Verified facts (do not re-research)
- Inline URL convention is in upstream JSONs:
  - workflow_corpus/official/video/wan_t2v.json:38 — text_encoders/umt5_xxl_fp8_e4m3fn_scaled.safetensors
  - workflow_corpus/official/video/wan_t2v.json:79 — vae/wan_2.1_vae.safetensors
  - workflow_corpus/official/video/wan_t2v.json:200 — diffusion_models/wan2.1_t2v_1.3B_fp16.safetensors
  - The URL field lives at the same JSON level as `class_type` and `inputs` for loader nodes.
- Materializer entry: `scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py` (writes `READY_REQUIREMENTS = {...}` literal into each generated scratchpad).
- Scratchpad shape: `ready_templates/{image,video,edit}/<name>.py` declares `API_WORKFLOW`, `READY_METADATA`, `READY_REQUIREMENTS`, and `build()`.
- Existing CLI registration: `vibecomfy/cli.py` + per-command modules under `vibecomfy/commands/` (e.g. `commands/doctor.py`).
- Existing dep is `httpx>=0.25` (pyproject.toml). No `huggingface_hub` dep — keep it that way for v1.

# Steps

## S1: Extract URLs during materialization
- In `scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py`, walk the source JSON and collect `(node_id, url)` entries from any node that has a top-level `"url"` field.
- For each URL, parse it to derive:
  - `name`: the basename (e.g. `wan2.1_t2v_1.3B_fp16.safetensors`)
  - `subdir`: the ComfyUI models subdir to land it in. Derive from the URL path — for HF Comfy-Org `split_files/<subdir>/<name>` URLs, use `<subdir>` (text_encoders, diffusion_models, vae, clip_vision, loras, latent_upscale_models, etc.). For other URL shapes, fall back to a heuristic based on the loader's `class_type` (CheckpointLoaderSimple → `checkpoints`, CLIPLoader → `text_encoders`, VAELoader → `vae`, LoraLoader → `loras`, ControlNetLoader → `controlnet`, UNETLoader → `diffusion_models`).
  - `url`: the URL as-is (drop `?download=true` query param if present).
- Emit `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]` as a list of `{"name": ..., "url": ..., "subdir": ...}` dicts in materialization order, deduplicated by `(name, subdir)`.
- Re-materialize all existing scratchpads as part of this step.

## S2: vibecomfy.fetch module
- New file `vibecomfy/fetch.py`:
  - `models_root() -> Path` — discover ComfyUI's models root via `comfy.cmd.folder_paths.folder_names_and_paths` if importable, else fall back to `os.environ["COMFY_MODELS_ROOT"]`, else `Path("ComfyUI/models")`.
  - `local_path(entry: dict) -> Path` — `models_root() / entry["subdir"] / entry["name"]`.
  - `is_present(entry: dict) -> bool` — checks the local path exists and has nonzero size.
  - `download(entry: dict, *, force: bool = False) -> Path` — uses `httpx.stream("GET", url, follow_redirects=True)` to download to a `.tmp` sibling file, then rename atomically. Raises `RuntimeError` with a clear message on 401/403 (license gating) and on 404. Skips if `is_present(entry)` and not `force`.
  - `download_many(entries: list[dict], *, force: bool = False) -> list[Path]` — sequential for v1 (parallel is a follow-up). Reports per-entry status via stdout (one line per entry: skipped/downloaded/failed).
- No `huggingface_hub` dep. Use plain HTTP + `Authorization: Bearer $HF_TOKEN` if the env var is set (for license-gated repos).

## S3: vibecomfy fetch CLI command
- New file `vibecomfy/commands/fetch.py`:
  - `vibecomfy fetch <workflow>` — resolves the workflow (same resolution as `inspect`/`doctor`), reads `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]`, calls `download_many`, reports counts.
  - Flags: `--force` (re-download present), `--dry-run` (print what would be fetched).
  - Register in `vibecomfy/cli.py` alongside the other commands.

## S4: doctor integration
- Extend `vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py` to:
  - Read `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]` from the resolved workflow.
  - For each entry, call `is_present`. Report missing entries with name + url + expected local path.
  - Exit nonzero (or surface a flag in the existing doctor output schema) when models are missing.

## S5: Tests
- `tests/test_materialize_extraction.py` — unit-test the URL/subdir derivation against a small fixture JSON (built inline, not snapshot) plus one real upstream sample.
- `tests/test_fetch.py` — mock httpx to verify: skip-when-present, download-to-tmp-then-rename, 401/404 error messages, dry-run.
- `tests/test_doctor_models.py` — verify doctor reports missing models from a fake `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]` and a tmp models root.
- `tests/test_fetch_cli.py` — argparse + dispatch.

# Out of scope (do NOT add)
- Bundle/manifest format (.toml manifest, vibe pack zip, install command). Separate plan.
- SHA256 verification. HF doesn't reliably expose hashes via resolve URLs; first-run hash + cache is a follow-up.
- Civitai-specific auth / API key handling.
- Parallel/multi-connection downloads.
- Auto-fetch on `vibecomfy run` (must be explicit `vibecomfy fetch`).
- A registry / hub for sharing.
- huggingface_hub dependency.

# Design constraints
- Default to HF, but the downloader is URL-driven, not provider-driven. A Civitai/R2/S3 URL works too — only the subdir derivation has an HF-aware fast path.
- Strip `?download=true` query params (Comfy-Org sometimes appends these).
- Strip `split_files/` prefix when deriving subdir from HF Comfy-Org URLs.
- License-gated repos (FLUX.2-klein-base-9b is one): download() returns a clear error pointing at the URL when 401/403, doesn't try to handle the agreement flow.
- Atomic writes (rename from .tmp). No partial files left behind on Ctrl-C.
- No new top-level deps. httpx is already there.
- Sequential downloads; this is v1 not perf-critical.

# Open questions
- Should `vibecomfy fetch` accept a `--workflow` group flag for multi-workflow batch fetching, or stick to one workflow per invocation? Lean toward one per invocation; users can `xargs` if they want.
- Should the materializer also extract custom-node references (already in `READY_REQUIREMENTS["custom_nodes"]` but as bare names)? Out of scope here — we don't have URLs for those in the JSON.
- Should `models_root()` honor a vibecomfy-specific env var like `VIBECOMFY_MODELS_ROOT` in addition to `COMFY_MODELS_ROOT`? Yes — fall through both before the hardcoded default.

  "meta_commentary": "Execution order is tight: T2 (`_merge_requirements` tolerance) MUST land before T3 re-materialises any scratchpad, because a freshly-materialised scratchpad with `list[dict]` models would crash the existing dict-sort path on import. Land T2 first even though it conceptually belongs with the materializer changes.\n\nFLAG-003 is the most important open concern \u2014 the plan body literally says `httpx.stream('GET', ...)` but also passes `client: httpx.Client | None`. Implement it as a real branch: `if client is not None: stream_ctx = client.stream('GET', url, follow_redirects=True, headers=headers, timeout=...)` else `stream_ctx = httpx.stream(...)`. Without this, the test injection point is dead and the mocked-streaming tests will be brittle/awkward.\n\nThe FLUX2 GGUF gap is intentional. Don't try to resolve it. The plan's policy is: leave it unresolved in v1, document inline in `_apply_flux2_gguf_policy`, surface the gap via the smoke test. A follow-up plan will populate `model_assets_extra` with the chosen URL once a canonical source is picked.\n\nFor the post-policy filter (T3), the FLUX2 case is the canonical test: the source JSON advertises `flux-2-klein-base-9b-fp8.safetensors`, `qwen_3_8b_fp8mixed.safetensors`, `full_encoder_small_decoder.safetensors`, but the policy renames the diffusion UNET to `flux-2-klein-9b-Q4_K_M.gguf` and the VAE to `flux2-vae.safetensors`. After the filter, only the qwen text encoder should survive (its filename is unchanged by policy). Verify by reading the regenerated `ready_templates/image/flux2_klein_9b_gguf_t2i.py`.\n\nThe walk order in T1 matters for determinism. Sort top-level nodes by `int(node['id'])` (handle string ids that look like ints), then visit subgraphs in definition order, then within each subgraph also sort by `int(node['id'])`. Subgraph node IDs are NOT globally unique across nesting levels \u2014 the dedup key is `(name, subdir)`, not node id.\n\nDon't add `huggingface_hub`, `tqdm`, or any progress library. httpx + plain prints is the v1 contract.\n\nDon't auto-fetch on `vibecomfy run`. The CLI command is explicit.\n\nWatch for incidental regressions in `tests/test_ready_templates.py` after re-materialisation. The plan claims `test_ready_template_preserves_materialized_requirements` only asserts custom_nodes \u2014 verify that assumption when the test runs (T7 step 3); if it actually inspects `models`, you may need to update it to accept the new shape.\n\nDon't create a new test file for the throwaway repro in T7 \u2014 write it to `/tmp/repro_<thing>.py`, run it, then delete it. The user's instructions explicitly say to delete it after confirming the fix.\n\nFor the `models_root()` `comfy.cmd.folder_paths` probe in T4 \u2014 wrap the entire import-and-lookup in `try: ... except Exception:` so that absence of comfy or any AttributeError silently falls through to the next layer. Don't let an import error leak to callers.",

- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

Any]) -> list[dict[str, str]]:\n+    \"\"\"Extract structured model asset entries from a ComfyUI workflow JSON object.\"\"\"\n+\n+    if not isinstance(raw, Mapping):\n+        return []\n+    has_nodes = bool(raw.get(\"nodes\"))\n+    has_subgraphs = bool(_subgraphs(raw))\n+    if not has_nodes and not has_subgraphs:\n+        return []\n+\n+    entries: list[dict[str, str]] = []\n+    seen: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()\n+    for node in _iter_workflow_nodes(raw):\n+        for entry in _entries_from_node(node):\n+            key = (entry[\"name\"], entry[\"subdir\"])\n+            if key in seen:\n+                continue\n+            seen.add(key)\n+            entries.append(entry)\n+    return entries\n+\n+\n+def entries_from_scratchpad_path(path: str | Path) -> list[dict[str, str]]:\n+    \"\"\"Read structured model assets from a materialized ready scratchpad.\"\"\"\n+\n+    module_ast = ast.parse(Path(path).read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"), filename=str(path))\n+    for node in module_ast.body:\n+        if not isinstance(node, ast.Assign):\n+            continue\n+        if not any(isinstance(target, ast.Name) and target.id == \"READY_REQUIREMENTS\" for target in node.targets):\n+            continue\n+        requirements = ast.literal_eval(node.value)\n+        if not isinstance(requirements, Mapping):\n+            return []\n+        models = requirements.get(\"models\", [])\n+        if not isinstance(models, list):\n+            return []\n+        return _normalise_requirement_entries(models)\n+    return []\n+\n+\n+def _entries_from_node(node: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Iterable[dict[str, str]]:\n+    properties = node.get(\"properties\", {})\n+    if not isinstance(properties, Mapping):\n+        return []\n+    models = properties.get(\"models\", [])\n+    if not isinstance(models, list):\n+        return []\n+    class_type = _node_class_type(node)\n+    return (_normalise_model_entry(model, class_type=class_type) for model in models)\n+\n+\n+def _normalise_requirement_entries(models: Iterable[Any]) -> list[dict[str, str]]:\n+    entries: list[dict[str, str]] = []\n+    seen: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()\n+    for model in models:\n+        if not isinstance(model, Mapping):\n+            continue\n+        entry = _normalise_model_entry(model, class_type=\"\")\n+        if entry is None:\n+            continue\n+        key = (entry[\"name\"], entry[\"subdir\"])\n+        if key in seen:\n+            continue\n+        seen.add(key)\n+        entries.append(entry)\n+    return entries\n+\n+\n+def _normalise_model_entry(model: Any, *, class_type: str) -> dict[str, str] | None:\n+    if not isinstance(model, Mapping):\n+        return None\n+    name = model.get(\"name\")\n+    url = model.get(\"url\")\n+    if not isinstance(name, str) or not name:\n+        return None\n+    if not isinstance(url, str) or not url:\n+        return None\n+    subdir = _subdir_for_model(model, class_type=class_type, url=url)\n+    return {\"name\": name, \"url\": _strip_download_true(url), \"subdir\": subdir}\n+\n+\n+def _subdir_for_model(model: Mapping[str, Any], *, class_type: str, url: str) -> str:\n+    directory = model.get(\"directory\")\n+    if isinstance(directory, str) and directory:\n+        return directory\n+    split_subdir = _hf_split_files_subdir(url)\n+    if split_subdir is not None:\n+        return split_subdir\n+    return _CLASS_TYPE_SUBDIRS.get(class_type, \"checkpoints\")\n+\n+\n+def _hf_split_files_subdir(url: str) -> str | None:\n+    path = urlsplit(url).path\n+    match = _HF_SPLIT_FILES_RE.search(path)\n+    if not match:\n+        return None\n+    subdir = match.group(1)\n+    if subdir not in HF_SPLIT_FILES_DIRS:\n+        return None\n+    return subdir\n+\n+\n+def _strip_download_true(url: str) -> str:\n+    parsed = urlsplit(url)\n+    params = [(key, value) for key, value in parse_qsl(parsed.query, keep_blank_values=True) if not (key == \"download\" and value == \"true\")]\n+    return urlunsplit((parsed.scheme, parsed.netloc, parsed.path, parsed.query and urlencode(params), parsed.fragment))\n+\n+\n+def _iter_workflow_nodes(raw: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Iterable[Mapping[str, Any]]:\n+    yield from _sorted_nodes(raw.get(\"nodes\"))\n+    for subgraph in _subgraphs(raw):\n+        yield from _iter_workflow_nodes(subgraph)\n+\n+\n+def _subgraphs(raw: Mapping[str, Any]) -> list[Mapping[str, Any]]:\n+    definitions = raw.get(\"definitions\", {})\n+    if not isinstance(definitions, Mapping):\n+        return []\n+    subgraphs = definitions.get(\"subgraphs\", [])\n+    if not isinstance(subgraphs, list):\n+        return []\n+    return [subgraph for subgraph in subgraphs if isinstance(subgraph, Mapping)]\n+\n+\n+def _sorted_nodes(nodes: Any) -> list[Mapping[str, Any]]:\n+    if isinstance(nodes, Mapping):\n+        values = nodes.values()\n+    elif isinstance(nodes, list):\n+        values = nodes\n+    else:\n+        return []\n+    return sorted(\n+        [node for node in values if isinstance(node, Mapping)],\n+        key=lambda node: _node_sort_key(node.get(\"id\")),\n+    )\n+\n+\n+def _node_sort_key(node_id: Any) -> tuple[int, str]:\n+    try:\n+        return (int(node_id), str(node_id))\n+    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n+        return (10**12, str(node_id))\n+\n+\n+def _node_class_type(node: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:\n+    for key in (\"class_type\", \"type\"):\n+        value = node.get(key)\n+        if isinstance(value, str) and value:\n+            return value\n+    properties = node.get(\"properties\", {})\n+    if isinstance(properties, Mapping):\n+        value = properties.get(\"Node name for S&R\")\n+        if isinstance(value, str) and value:\n+            return value\n+    return \"\"\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\"HF_SPLIT_FILES_DIRS\", \"entries_from_scratchpad_path\", \"extract_from_raw_workflow\"]\n*** End Patch\n"
}

        Idea:
vibecomfy: model-distribution v1 — extract HuggingFace URLs from workflow JSONs, populate READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"], add a small fetch CLI and doctor integration. Don't build a bundle/manifest format yet.

Source repo: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy

# Problem
Today vibecomfy has a half-built model-distribution story:
- Upstream Comfy-Org workflow JSONs in `workflow_corpus/official/*.json` already carry inline `"url": "https://huggingface.co/..."` fields per model-loader node (see workflow_corpus/official/video/wan_t2v.json:38, ltx2_3_i2v.json:699, flux2_klein_4b_t2i.json:1082, etc.) plus markdown notes in human-readable form.
- The materializer in `scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py` builds `ready_templates/*/*.py` from those JSONs but never extracts the URLs — every scratchpad has `READY_REQUIREMENTS = {"models": [], "custom_nodes": [...]}` with `models` empty.
- There is no downloader. `vibecomfy doctor` checks runtime readiness but doesn't check model presence.
- The de-facto convention is HuggingFace via the upstream Comfy-Org URL fields, but nothing in vibecomfy reads or enforces it.

# Goal
Close the loop in five mechanical steps:
1. Materializer extracts URLs from workflow JSON and populates `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]` with structured entries.
2. A small `vibecomfy.fetch` module downloads missing models from URLs to the right ComfyUI subdir.
3. `vibecomfy fetch <workflow>` CLI command resolves a workflow's model list and downloads the missing ones.
4. `vibecomfy doctor <workflow>` reports missing models with their URLs.
5. Tests for extraction, fetch (mocked httpx), and doctor.

The bundle/manifest format and SHA pinning are explicitly OUT of scope — they're a separate plan.

# Verified facts (do not re-research)
- Inline URL convention is in upstream JSONs:
  - workflow_corpus/official/video/wan_t2v.json:38 — text_encoders/umt5_xxl_fp8_e4m3fn_scaled.safetensors
  - workflow_corpus/official/video/wan_t2v.json:79 — vae/wan_2.1_vae.safetensors
  - workflow_corpus/official/video/wan_t2v.json:200 — diffusion_models/wan2.1_t2v_1.3B_fp16.safetensors
  - The URL field lives at the same JSON level as `class_type` and `inputs` for loader nodes.
- Materializer entry: `scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py` (writes `READY_REQUIREMENTS = {...}` literal into each generated scratchpad).
- Scratchpad shape: `ready_templates/{image,video,edit}/<name>.py` declares `API_WORKFLOW`, `READY_METADATA`, `READY_REQUIREMENTS`, and `build()`.
- Existing CLI registration: `vibecomfy/cli.py` + per-command modules under `vibecomfy/commands/` (e.g. `commands/doctor.py`).
- Existing dep is `httpx>=0.25` (pyproject.toml). No `huggingface_hub` dep — keep it that way for v1.

# Steps

## S1: Extract URLs during materialization
- In `scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py`, walk the source JSON and collect `(node_id, url)` entries from any node that has a top-level `"url"` field.
- For each URL, parse it to derive:
  - `name`: the basename (e.g. `wan2.1_t2v_1.3B_fp16.safetensors`)
  - `subdir`: the ComfyUI models subdir to land it in. Derive from the URL path — for HF Comfy-Org `split_files/<subdir>/<name>` URLs, use `<subdir>` (text_encoders, diffusion_models, vae, clip_vision, loras, latent_upscale_models, etc.). For other URL shapes, fall back to a heuristic based on the loader's `class_type` (CheckpointLoaderSimple → `checkpoints`, CLIPLoader → `text_encoders`, VAELoader → `vae`, LoraLoader → `loras`, ControlNetLoader → `controlnet`, UNETLoader → `diffusion_models`).
  - `url`: the URL as-is (drop `?download=true` query param if present).
- Emit `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]` as a list of `{"name": ..., "url": ..., "subdir": ...}` dicts in materialization order, deduplicated by `(name, subdir)`.
- Re-materialize all existing scratchpads as part of this step.

## S2: vibecomfy.fetch module
- New file `vibecomfy/fetch.py`:
  - `models_root() -> Path` — discover ComfyUI's models root via `comfy.cmd.folder_paths.folder_names_and_paths` if importable, else fall back to `os.environ["COMFY_MODELS_ROOT"]`, else `Path("ComfyUI/models")`.
  - `local_path(entry: dict) -> Path` — `models_root() / entry["subdir"] / entry["name"]`.
  - `is_present(entry: dict) -> bool` — checks the local path exists and has nonzero size.
  - `download(entry: dict, *, force: bool = False) -> Path` — uses `httpx.stream("GET", url, follow_redirects=True)` to download to a `.tmp` sibling file, then rename atomically. Raises `RuntimeError` with a clear message on 401/403 (license gating) and on 404. Skips if `is_present(entry)` and not `force`.
  - `download_many(entries: list[dict], *, force: bool = False) -> list[Path]` — sequential for v1 (parallel is a follow-up). Reports per-entry status via stdout (one line per entry: skipped/downloaded/failed).
- No `huggingface_hub` dep. Use plain HTTP + `Authorization: Bearer $HF_TOKEN` if the env var is set (for license-gated repos).

## S3: vibecomfy fetch CLI command
- New file `vibecomfy/commands/fetch.py`:
  - `vibecomfy fetch <workflow>` — resolves the workflow (same resolution as `inspect`/`doctor`), reads `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]`, calls `download_many`, reports counts.
  - Flags: `--force` (re-download present), `--dry-run` (print what would be fetched).
  - Register in `vibecomfy/cli.py` alongside the other commands.

## S4: doctor integration
- Extend `vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py` to:
  - Read `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]` from the resolved workflow.
  - For each entry, call `is_present`. Report missing entries with name + url + expected local path.
  - Exit nonzero (or surface a flag in the existing doctor output schema) when models are missing.

## S5: Tests
- `tests/test_materialize_extraction.py` — unit-test the URL/subdir derivation against a small fixture JSON (built inline, not snapshot) plus one real upstream sample.
- `tests/test_fetch.py` — mock httpx to verify: skip-when-present, download-to-tmp-then-rename, 401/404 error messages, dry-run.
- `tests/test_doctor_models.py` — verify doctor reports missing models from a fake `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]` and a tmp models root.
- `tests/test_fetch_cli.py` — argparse + dispatch.

# Out of scope (do NOT add)
- Bundle/manifest format (.toml manifest, vibe pack zip, install command). Separate plan.
- SHA256 verification. HF doesn't reliably expose hashes via resolve URLs; first-run hash + cache is a follow-up.
- Civitai-specific auth / API key handling.
- Parallel/multi-connection downloads.
- Auto-fetch on `vibecomfy run` (must be explicit `vibecomfy fetch`).
- A registry / hub for sharing.
- huggingface_hub dependency.

# Design constraints
- Default to HF, but the downloader is URL-driven, not provider-driven. A Civitai/R2/S3 URL works too — only the subdir derivation has an HF-aware fast path.
- Strip `?download=true` query params (Comfy-Org sometimes appends these).
- Strip `split_files/` prefix when deriving subdir from HF Comfy-Org URLs.
- License-gated repos (FLUX.2-klein-base-9b is one): download() returns a clear error pointing at the URL when 401/403, doesn't try to handle the agreement flow.
- Atomic writes (rename from .tmp). No partial files left behind on Ctrl-C.
- No new top-level deps. httpx is already there.
- Sequential downloads; this is v1 not perf-critical.

# Open questions
- Should `vibecomfy fetch` accept a `--workflow` group flag for multi-workflow batch fetching, or stick to one workflow per invocation? Lean toward one per invocation; users can `xargs` if they want.
- Should the materializer also extract custom-node references (already in `READY_REQUIREMENTS["custom_nodes"]` but as bare names)? Out of scope here — we don't have URLs for those in the JSON.
- Should `models_root()` honor a vibecomfy-specific env var like `VIBECOMFY_MODELS_ROOT` in addition to `COMFY_MODELS_ROOT`? Yes — fall through both before the hardcoded default.

(c) Add `_finalise_model_assets(workflow)` invoked inside `_write_ready_template` AFTER all policies have applied: read raw-extracted `workflow.metadata.get('model_assets', [])`, read `workflow.metadata.get('model_assets_extra', [])` (treated as authoritative \u2014 no filter), filter the raw-extracted list to entries whose `name` \u2208 `_referenced_model_filenames(workflow)`, concatenate `filtered + extras`, dedup by `(name, subdir)` preserving first-seen order, store back as `workflow.metadata['model_assets']`. (d) Change `_write_ready_template` (~lines 237-240) so the literal `READY_REQUIREMENTS` written to disk is `{'models': workflow.metadata.get('model_assets', []), 'custom_nodes': sorted(workflow.requirements.custom_nodes)}` \u2014 models stay in materialisation order (no sort), custom_nodes still sorted. (e) Add an inline comment in `_apply_flux2_gguf_policy` documenting that `flux-2-klein-9b-Q4_K_M.gguf` has no canonical Comfy-Org URL and must be supplied via `workflow.metadata['model_assets_extra']` by a follow-up \u2014 do NOT fabricate a URL. (f) Re-materialise all scratchpads via `uv run python scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py`. Spot-check: `ready_templates/video/wan_t2v.py` shows 3 entries; `ready_templates/video/ltx2_3_t2v.py` shows fp8/fp4 entries; `ready_templates/image/flux2_klein_9b_gguf_t2i.py` shows surviving subgraph URLs (qwen_3_8b text encoder remains; diffusion/VAE entries filtered out). (a) `models_root() -> Path`: fall through `os.environ.get('VIBECOMFY_MODELS_ROOT')` \u2192 `os.environ.get('COMFY_MODELS_ROOT')` \u2192 try `from comfy.cmd.folder_paths import folder_names_and_paths; folder_names_and_paths['checkpoints'][0][0]`'s parent (best-effort, wrapped in try/except) \u2192 `Path('ComfyUI/models')`. No filesystem touch beyond reading env. (b) `local_path(entry)` \u2192 `models_root() / entry['subdir'] / entry['name']`. (c) `is_present(entry)` \u2192 `path.is_file() and path.stat().st_size > 0`. (d) `download(entry, *, force=False, client=None) -> Path`: skip-if-present prints `'skipped {name}'`; build headers, add `Authorization: Bearer {HF_TOKEN}` only if env set; strip `?download=true` from URL; use `client.stream('GET', url, ...)` when `client` is supplied (FLAG-003: do NOT call `httpx.stream` when a client is passed) else `httpx.stream('GET', url, follow_redirects=True, headers=headers, timeout=httpx.Timeout(60, read=None))`; on 401/403 raise `PermissionError(f'License-gated download blocked for {url} \u2014 set HF_TOKEN or accept the license at the source URL.')`; on 404 raise `FileNotFoundError(f'Asset not found at {url}')`; other non-2xx \u2192 `RuntimeError(f'HTTP {status} fetching {url}')`. Stream into `path.with_suffix(path.suffix + '.tmp')`, mkdir parent, then `os.replace(tmp, path)`. Wrap streaming in try/except (including KeyboardInterrupt) that calls `tmp.unlink(missing_ok=True)` before re-raising, so no `.tmp` is left behind. (e) `download_many(entries, *, force=False) -> list[Path]`: sequential; per-entry print one line (`skipped <name>` / `downloaded <name> -> <path>` / `failed <name>: <error>`); continue past failures; raise an aggregate `RuntimeError('N failures')` only at the end so partial progress is preserved. NO new pyproject dep \u2014 httpx already declared. Add `tests/test_fetch.py` covering: `monkeypatch.setenv('VIBECOMFY_MODELS_ROOT', tmp_path)`; skip-when-present; atomic rename (stub `httpx.stream` with a contextmanager returning a fake response with `iter_bytes` and `status_code=200`, assert no `.tmp` remains and final path has expected bytes); 401\u2192PermissionError and 404\u2192FileNotFoundError messages include URL; HF_TOKEN env triggers `Authorization` header (and absence omits it); `?download=true` is stripped from the requested URL; `download_many` continues past failure and raises aggregate `RuntimeError`.",

A follow-up plan will populate `model_assets_extra` with the chosen URL once a canonical source is picked.\n\nFor the post-policy filter (T3), the FLUX2 case is the canonical test: the source JSON advertises `flux-2-klein-base-9b-fp8.safetensors`, `qwen_3_8b_fp8mixed.safetensors`, `full_encoder_small_decoder.safetensors`, but the policy renames the diffusion UNET to `flux-2-klein-9b-Q4_K_M.gguf` and the VAE to `flux2-vae.safetensors`. - [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy

        Idea:
vibecomfy: model-distribution v1 — extract HuggingFace URLs from workflow JSONs, populate READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"], add a small fetch CLI and doctor integration. Don't build a bundle/manifest format yet.

Source repo: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy

# Problem
Today vibecomfy has a half-built model-distribution story:
- Upstream Comfy-Org workflow JSONs in `workflow_corpus/official/*.json` already carry inline `"url": "https://huggingface.co/..."` fields per model-loader node (see workflow_corpus/official/video/wan_t2v.json:38, ltx2_3_i2v.json:699, flux2_klein_4b_t2i.json:1082, etc.) plus markdown notes in human-readable form.
- The materializer in `scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py` builds `ready_templates/*/*.py` from those JSONs but never extracts the URLs — every scratchpad has `READY_REQUIREMENTS = {"models": [], "custom_nodes": [...]}` with `models` empty.
- There is no downloader. `vibecomfy doctor` checks runtime readiness but doesn't check model presence.
- The de-facto convention is HuggingFace via the upstream Comfy-Org URL fields, but nothing in vibecomfy reads or enforces it.

# Goal
Close the loop in five mechanical steps:
1. Materializer extracts URLs from workflow JSON and populates `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]` with structured entries.
2. A small `vibecomfy.fetch` module downloads missing models from URLs to the right ComfyUI subdir.
3. `vibecomfy fetch <workflow>` CLI command resolves a workflow's model list and downloads the missing ones.
4. `vibecomfy doctor <workflow>` reports missing models with their URLs.
5. Tests for extraction, fetch (mocked httpx), and doctor.

The bundle/manifest format and SHA pinning are explicitly OUT of scope — they're a separate plan.

# Verified facts (do not re-research)
- Inline URL convention is in upstream JSONs:
  - workflow_corpus/official/video/wan_t2v.json:38 — text_encoders/umt5_xxl_fp8_e4m3fn_scaled.safetensors
  - workflow_corpus/official/video/wan_t2v.json:79 — vae/wan_2.1_vae.safetensors
  - workflow_corpus/official/video/wan_t2v.json:200 — diffusion_models/wan2.1_t2v_1.3B_fp16.safetensors
  - The URL field lives at the same JSON level as `class_type` and `inputs` for loader nodes.
- Materializer entry: `scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py` (writes `READY_REQUIREMENTS = {...}` literal into each generated scratchpad).
- Scratchpad shape: `ready_templates/{image,video,edit}/<name>.py` declares `API_WORKFLOW`, `READY_METADATA`, `READY_REQUIREMENTS`, and `build()`.
- Existing CLI registration: `vibecomfy/cli.py` + per-command modules under `vibecomfy/commands/` (e.g. `commands/doctor.py`).
- Existing dep is `httpx>=0.25` (pyproject.toml). No `huggingface_hub` dep — keep it that way for v1.

# Steps

## S1: Extract URLs during materialization
- In `scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py`, walk the source JSON and collect `(node_id, url)` entries from any node that has a top-level `"url"` field.
- For each URL, parse it to derive:
  - `name`: the basename (e.g. `wan2.1_t2v_1.3B_fp16.safetensors`)
  - `subdir`: the ComfyUI models subdir to land it in. Derive from the URL path — for HF Comfy-Org `split_files/<subdir>/<name>` URLs, use `<subdir>` (text_encoders, diffusion_models, vae, clip_vision, loras, latent_upscale_models, etc.). For other URL shapes, fall back to a heuristic based on the loader's `class_type` (CheckpointLoaderSimple → `checkpoints`, CLIPLoader → `text_encoders`, VAELoader → `vae`, LoraLoader → `loras`, ControlNetLoader → `controlnet`, UNETLoader → `diffusion_models`).
  - `url`: the URL as-is (drop `?download=true` query param if present).
- Emit `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]` as a list of `{"name": ..., "url": ..., "subdir": ...}` dicts in materialization order, deduplicated by `(name, subdir)`.
- Re-materialize all existing scratchpads as part of this step.

## S2: vibecomfy.fetch module
- New file `vibecomfy/fetch.py`:
  - `models_root() -> Path` — discover ComfyUI's models root via `comfy.cmd.folder_paths.folder_names_and_paths` if importable, else fall back to `os.environ["COMFY_MODELS_ROOT"]`, else `Path("ComfyUI/models")`.
  - `local_path(entry: dict) -> Path` — `models_root() / entry["subdir"] / entry["name"]`.
  - `is_present(entry: dict) -> bool` — checks the local path exists and has nonzero size.
  - `download(entry: dict, *, force: bool = False) -> Path` — uses `httpx.stream("GET", url, follow_redirects=True)` to download to a `.tmp` sibling file, then rename atomically. Raises `RuntimeError` with a clear message on 401/403 (license gating) and on 404. Skips if `is_present(entry)` and not `force`.
  - `download_many(entries: list[dict], *, force: bool = False) -> list[Path]` — sequential for v1 (parallel is a follow-up). Reports per-entry status via stdout (one line per entry: skipped/downloaded/failed).
- No `huggingface_hub` dep. Use plain HTTP + `Authorization: Bearer $HF_TOKEN` if the env var is set (for license-gated repos).

## S3: vibecomfy fetch CLI command
- New file `vibecomfy/commands/fetch.py`:
  - `vibecomfy fetch <workflow>` — resolves the workflow (same resolution as `inspect`/`doctor`), reads `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]`, calls `download_many`, reports counts.
  - Flags: `--force` (re-download present), `--dry-run` (print what would be fetched).
  - Register in `vibecomfy/cli.py` alongside the other commands.

## S4: doctor integration
- Extend `vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py` to:
  - Read `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]` from the resolved workflow.
  - For each entry, call `is_present`. Report missing entries with name + url + expected local path.
  - Exit nonzero (or surface a flag in the existing doctor output schema) when models are missing.

## S5: Tests
- `tests/test_materialize_extraction.py` — unit-test the URL/subdir derivation against a small fixture JSON (built inline, not snapshot) plus one real upstream sample.
- `tests/test_fetch.py` — mock httpx to verify: skip-when-present, download-to-tmp-then-rename, 401/404 error messages, dry-run.
- `tests/test_doctor_models.py` — verify doctor reports missing models from a fake `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]` and a tmp models root.
- `tests/test_fetch_cli.py` — argparse + dispatch.

# Out of scope (do NOT add)
- Bundle/manifest format (.toml manifest, vibe pack zip, install command). Separate plan.
- SHA256 verification. HF doesn't reliably expose hashes via resolve URLs; first-run hash + cache is a follow-up.
- Civitai-specific auth / API key handling.
- Parallel/multi-connection downloads.
- Auto-fetch on `vibecomfy run` (must be explicit `vibecomfy fetch`).
- A registry / hub for sharing.
- huggingface_hub dependency.

# Design constraints
- Default to HF, but the downloader is URL-driven, not provider-driven. A Civitai/R2/S3 URL works too — only the subdir derivation has an HF-aware fast path.
- Strip `?download=true` query params (Comfy-Org sometimes appends these).
- Strip `split_files/` prefix when deriving subdir from HF Comfy-Org URLs.
- License-gated repos (FLUX.2-klein-base-9b is one): download() returns a clear error pointing at the URL when 401/403, doesn't try to handle the agreement flow.
- Atomic writes (rename from .tmp). No partial files left behind on Ctrl-C.
- No new top-level deps. httpx is already there.
- Sequential downloads; this is v1 not perf-critical.

# Open questions
- Should `vibecomfy fetch` accept a `--workflow` group flag for multi-workflow batch fetching, or stick to one workflow per invocation? Lean toward one per invocation; users can `xargs` if they want.
- Should the materializer also extract custom-node references (already in `READY_REQUIREMENTS["custom_nodes"]` but as bare names)? Out of scope here — we don't have URLs for those in the JSON.
- Should `models_root()` honor a vibecomfy-specific env var like `VIBECOMFY_MODELS_ROOT` in addition to `COMFY_MODELS_ROOT`? Yes — fall through both before the hardcoded default.

      "id": "T4",
      "description": "Create `vibecomfy/fetch.py` with the downloader. (a) `models_root() -> Path`: fall through `os.environ.get('VIBECOMFY_MODELS_ROOT')` \u2192 `os.environ.get('COMFY_MODELS_ROOT')` \u2192 try `from comfy.cmd.folder_paths import folder_names_and_paths; folder_names_and_paths['checkpoints'][0][0]`'s parent (best-effort, wrapped in try/except) \u2192 `Path('ComfyUI/models')`. No filesystem touch beyond reading env. (b) `local_path(entry)` \u2192 `models_root() / entry['subdir'] / entry['name']`. (c) `is_present(entry)` \u2192 `path.is_file() and path.stat().st_size > 0`. (d) `download(entry, *, force=False, client=None) -> Path`: skip-if-present prints `'skipped {name}'`; build headers, add `Authorization: Bearer {HF_TOKEN}` only if env set; strip `?download=true` from URL; use `client.stream('GET', url, ...)` when `client` is supplied (FLAG-003: do NOT call `httpx.stream` when a client is passed) else `httpx.stream('GET', url, follow_redirects=True, headers=headers, timeout=httpx.Timeout(60, read=None))`; on 401/403 raise `PermissionError(f'License-gated download blocked for {url} \u2014 set HF_TOKEN or accept the license at the source URL.')`; on 404 raise `FileNotFoundError(f'Asset not found at {url}')`; other non-2xx \u2192 `RuntimeError(f'HTTP {status} fetching {url}')`. Stream into `path.with_suffix(path.suffix + '.tmp')`, mkdir parent, then `os.replace(tmp, path)`. Wrap streaming in try/except (including KeyboardInterrupt) that calls `tmp.unlink(missing_ok=True)` before re-raising, so no `.tmp` is left behind. (e) `download_many(entries, *, force=False) -> list[Path]`: sequential; per-entry print one line (`skipped <name>` / `downloaded <name> -> <path>` / `failed <name>: <error>`); continue past failures; raise an aggregate `RuntimeError('N failures')` only at the end so partial progress is preserved. NO new pyproject dep \u2014 httpx already declared. Add `tests/test_fetch.py` covering: `monkeypatch.setenv('VIBECOMFY_MODELS_ROOT', tmp_path)`; skip-when-present; atomic rename (stub `httpx.stream` with a contextmanager returning a fake response with `iter_bytes` and `status_code=200`, assert no `.tmp` remains and final path has expected bytes); 401\u2192PermissionError and 404\u2192FileNotFoundError messages include URL; HF_TOKEN env triggers `Authorization` header (and absence omits it); `?download=true` is stripped from the requested URL; `download_many` continues past failure and raises aggregate `RuntimeError`.",

  "meta_commentary": "Execution order is tight: T2 (`_merge_requirements` tolerance) MUST land before T3 re-materialises any scratchpad, because a freshly-materialised scratchpad with `list[dict]` models would crash the existing dict-sort path on import. Land T2 first even though it conceptually belongs with the materializer changes.\n\nFLAG-003 is the most important open concern \u2014 the plan body literally says `httpx.stream('GET', ...)` but also passes `client: httpx.Client | None`. Implement it as a real branch: `if client is not None: stream_ctx = client.stream('GET', url, follow_redirects=True, headers=headers, timeout=...)` else `stream_ctx = httpx.stream(...)`. Without this, the test injection point is dead and the mocked-streaming tests will be brittle/awkward.\n\nThe FLUX2 GGUF gap is intentional. Don't try to resolve it. The plan's policy is: leave it unresolved in v1, document inline in `_apply_flux2_gguf_policy`, surface the gap via the smoke test. A follow-up plan will populate `model_assets_extra` with the chosen URL once a canonical source is picked.\n\nFor the post-policy filter (T3), the FLUX2 case is the canonical test: the source JSON advertises `flux-2-klein-base-9b-fp8.safetensors`, `qwen_3_8b_fp8mixed.safetensors`, `full_encoder_small_decoder.safetensors`, but the policy renames the diffusion UNET to `flux-2-klein-9b-Q4_K_M.gguf` and the VAE to `flux2-vae.safetensors`. After the filter, only the qwen text encoder should survive (its filename is unchanged by policy). Verify by reading the regenerated `ready_templates/image/flux2_klein_9b_gguf_t2i.py`.\n\nThe walk order in T1 matters for determinism. Sort top-level nodes by `int(node['id'])` (handle string ids that look like ints), then visit subgraphs in definition order, then within each subgraph also sort by `int(node['id'])`. Subgraph node IDs are NOT globally unique across nesting levels \u2014 the dedup key is `(name, subdir)`, not node id.\n\nDon't add `huggingface_hub`, `tqdm`, or any progress library. httpx + plain prints is the v1 contract.\n\nDon't auto-fetch on `vibecomfy run`. The CLI command is explicit.\n\nWatch for incidental regressions in `tests/test_ready_templates.py` after re-materialisation. The plan claims `test_ready_template_preserves_materialized_requirements` only asserts custom_nodes \u2014 verify that assumption when the test runs (T7 step 3); if it actually inspects `models`, you may need to update it to accept the new shape.\n\nDon't create a new test file for the throwaway repro in T7 \u2014 write it to `/tmp/repro_<thing>.py`, run it, then delete it. The user's instructions explicitly say to delete it after confirming the fix.\n\nFor the `models_root()` `comfy.cmd.folder_paths` probe in T4 \u2014 wrap the entire import-and-lookup in `try: ... except Exception:` so that absence of comfy or any AttributeError silently falls through to the next layer. Don't let an import error leak to callers.",

(flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

asset\n+        for asset in raw_assets\n+        if isinstance(asset, dict) and isinstance(asset.get(\"name\"), str) and asset[\"name\"] in referenced\n+    ]\n+    combined = filtered + [asset for asset in extra_assets if isinstance(asset, dict)]\n+    final_assets: list[dict] = []\n+    seen: set[tuple[object, object]] = set()\n+    for asset in combined:\n+        key = (asset.get(\"name\"), asset.get(\"subdir\"))\n+        if key in seen:\n+            continue\n+        seen.add(key)\n+        final_assets.append(asset)\n+    workflow.metadata[\"model_assets\"] = final_assets\n \n \n if __name__ == \"__main__\":\n*** End Patch\n"
}

{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py\n@@\n def _apply_flux2_gguf_policy(workflow: VibeWorkflow) -> None:\n+    workflow.metadata[\"model_assets_replaced_by_policy\"] = [\n+        \"flux-2-klein-base-9b-fp8.safetensors\",\n+        \"full_encoder_small_decoder.safetensors\",\n+    ]\n     for node in workflow.nodes.values():\n         if node.class_type == \"UNETLoader\" and \"unet_name\" in node.inputs:\n             node.class_type = \"UnetLoaderGGUF\"\n@@\n def _finalise_model_assets(workflow: VibeWorkflow) -> None:\n     referenced = _referenced_model_filenames(workflow)\n     raw_assets = workflow.metadata.get(\"model_assets\", [])\n     extra_assets = workflow.metadata.get(\"model_assets_extra\", [])\n+    replaced_by_policy = set(workflow.metadata.pop(\"model_assets_replaced_by_policy\", []))\n     filtered = [\n         asset\n         for asset in raw_assets\n-        if isinstance(asset, dict) and isinstance(asset.get(\"name\"), str) and asset[\"name\"] in referenced\n+        if isinstance(asset, dict)\n+        and isinstance(asset.get(\"name\"), str)\n+        and asset[\"name\"] not in replaced_by_policy\n+        and (asset[\"name\"] in referenced or _asset_name_appears_in_workflow_text(workflow, asset[\"name\"]))\n     ]\n@@\n     workflow.metadata[\"model_assets\"] = final_assets\n+\n+\n+def _asset_name_appears_in_workflow_text(workflow: VibeWorkflow, name: str) -> bool:\n+    for node in workflow.nodes.values():\n+        for value in list(node.inputs.values()) + list(node.widgets.values()):\n+            if isinstance(value, str) and name in value:\n+                return True\n+    return False\n*** End Patch\n"
}

        Idea:
vibecomfy: model-distribution v1 — extract HuggingFace URLs from workflow JSONs, populate READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"], add a small fetch CLI and doctor integration. Don't build a bundle/manifest format yet.

Source repo: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy

# Problem
Today vibecomfy has a half-built model-distribution story:
- Upstream Comfy-Org workflow JSONs in `workflow_corpus/official/*.json` already carry inline `"url": "https://huggingface.co/..."` fields per model-loader node (see workflow_corpus/official/video/wan_t2v.json:38, ltx2_3_i2v.json:699, flux2_klein_4b_t2i.json:1082, etc.) plus markdown notes in human-readable form.
- The materializer in `scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py` builds `ready_templates/*/*.py` from those JSONs but never extracts the URLs — every scratchpad has `READY_REQUIREMENTS = {"models": [], "custom_nodes": [...]}` with `models` empty.
- There is no downloader. `vibecomfy doctor` checks runtime readiness but doesn't check model presence.
- The de-facto convention is HuggingFace via the upstream Comfy-Org URL fields, but nothing in vibecomfy reads or enforces it.

# Goal
Close the loop in five mechanical steps:
1. Materializer extracts URLs from workflow JSON and populates `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]` with structured entries.
2. A small `vibecomfy.fetch` module downloads missing models from URLs to the right ComfyUI subdir.
3. `vibecomfy fetch <workflow>` CLI command resolves a workflow's model list and downloads the missing ones.
4. `vibecomfy doctor <workflow>` reports missing models with their URLs.
5. Tests for extraction, fetch (mocked httpx), and doctor.

The bundle/manifest format and SHA pinning are explicitly OUT of scope — they're a separate plan.

# Verified facts (do not re-research)
- Inline URL convention is in upstream JSONs:
  - workflow_corpus/official/video/wan_t2v.json:38 — text_encoders/umt5_xxl_fp8_e4m3fn_scaled.safetensors
  - workflow_corpus/official/video/wan_t2v.json:79 — vae/wan_2.1_vae.safetensors
  - workflow_corpus/official/video/wan_t2v.json:200 — diffusion_models/wan2.1_t2v_1.3B_fp16.safetensors
  - The URL field lives at the same JSON level as `class_type` and `inputs` for loader nodes.
- Materializer entry: `scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py` (writes `READY_REQUIREMENTS = {...}` literal into each generated scratchpad).
- Scratchpad shape: `ready_templates/{image,video,edit}/<name>.py` declares `API_WORKFLOW`, `READY_METADATA`, `READY_REQUIREMENTS`, and `build()`.
- Existing CLI registration: `vibecomfy/cli.py` + per-command modules under `vibecomfy/commands/` (e.g. `commands/doctor.py`).
- Existing dep is `httpx>=0.25` (pyproject.toml). No `huggingface_hub` dep — keep it that way for v1.

# Steps

## S1: Extract URLs during materialization
- In `scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py`, walk the source JSON and collect `(node_id, url)` entries from any node that has a top-level `"url"` field.
- For each URL, parse it to derive:
  - `name`: the basename (e.g. `wan2.1_t2v_1.3B_fp16.safetensors`)
  - `subdir`: the ComfyUI models subdir to land it in. Derive from the URL path — for HF Comfy-Org `split_files/<subdir>/<name>` URLs, use `<subdir>` (text_encoders, diffusion_models, vae, clip_vision, loras, latent_upscale_models, etc.). For other URL shapes, fall back to a heuristic based on the loader's `class_type` (CheckpointLoaderSimple → `checkpoints`, CLIPLoader → `text_encoders`, VAELoader → `vae`, LoraLoader → `loras`, ControlNetLoader → `controlnet`, UNETLoader → `diffusion_models`).
  - `url`: the URL as-is (drop `?download=true` query param if present).
- Emit `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]` as a list of `{"name": ..., "url": ..., "subdir": ...}` dicts in materialization order, deduplicated by `(name, subdir)`.
- Re-materialize all existing scratchpads as part of this step.

## S2: vibecomfy.fetch module
- New file `vibecomfy/fetch.py`:
  - `models_root() -> Path` — discover ComfyUI's models root via `comfy.cmd.folder_paths.folder_names_and_paths` if importable, else fall back to `os.environ["COMFY_MODELS_ROOT"]`, else `Path("ComfyUI/models")`.
  - `local_path(entry: dict) -> Path` — `models_root() / entry["subdir"] / entry["name"]`.
  - `is_present(entry: dict) -> bool` — checks the local path exists and has nonzero size.
  - `download(entry: dict, *, force: bool = False) -> Path` — uses `httpx.stream("GET", url, follow_redirects=True)` to download to a `.tmp` sibling file, then rename atomically. Raises `RuntimeError` with a clear message on 401/403 (license gating) and on 404. Skips if `is_present(entry)` and not `force`.
  - `download_many(entries: list[dict], *, force: bool = False) -> list[Path]` — sequential for v1 (parallel is a follow-up). Reports per-entry status via stdout (one line per entry: skipped/downloaded/failed).
- No `huggingface_hub` dep. Use plain HTTP + `Authorization: Bearer $HF_TOKEN` if the env var is set (for license-gated repos).

## S3: vibecomfy fetch CLI command
- New file `vibecomfy/commands/fetch.py`:
  - `vibecomfy fetch <workflow>` — resolves the workflow (same resolution as `inspect`/`doctor`), reads `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]`, calls `download_many`, reports counts.
  - Flags: `--force` (re-download present), `--dry-run` (print what would be fetched).
  - Register in `vibecomfy/cli.py` alongside the other commands.

## S4: doctor integration
- Extend `vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py` to:
  - Read `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]` from the resolved workflow.
  - For each entry, call `is_present`. Report missing entries with name + url + expected local path.
  - Exit nonzero (or surface a flag in the existing doctor output schema) when models are missing.

## S5: Tests
- `tests/test_materialize_extraction.py` — unit-test the URL/subdir derivation against a small fixture JSON (built inline, not snapshot) plus one real upstream sample.
- `tests/test_fetch.py` — mock httpx to verify: skip-when-present, download-to-tmp-then-rename, 401/404 error messages, dry-run.
- `tests/test_doctor_models.py` — verify doctor reports missing models from a fake `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]` and a tmp models root.
- `tests/test_fetch_cli.py` — argparse + dispatch.

# Out of scope (do NOT add)
- Bundle/manifest format (.toml manifest, vibe pack zip, install command). Separate plan.
- SHA256 verification. HF doesn't reliably expose hashes via resolve URLs; first-run hash + cache is a follow-up.
- Civitai-specific auth / API key handling.
- Parallel/multi-connection downloads.
- Auto-fetch on `vibecomfy run` (must be explicit `vibecomfy fetch`).
- A registry / hub for sharing.
- huggingface_hub dependency.

# Design constraints
- Default to HF, but the downloader is URL-driven, not provider-driven. A Civitai/R2/S3 URL works too — only the subdir derivation has an HF-aware fast path.
- Strip `?download=true` query params (Comfy-Org sometimes appends these).
- Strip `split_files/` prefix when deriving subdir from HF Comfy-Org URLs.
- License-gated repos (FLUX.2-klein-base-9b is one): download() returns a clear error pointing at the URL when 401/403, doesn't try to handle the agreement flow.
- Atomic writes (rename from .tmp). No partial files left behind on Ctrl-C.
- No new top-level deps. httpx is already there.
- Sequential downloads; this is v1 not perf-critical.

# Open questions
- Should `vibecomfy fetch` accept a `--workflow` group flag for multi-workflow batch fetching, or stick to one workflow per invocation? Lean toward one per invocation; users can `xargs` if they want.
- Should the materializer also extract custom-node references (already in `READY_REQUIREMENTS["custom_nodes"]` but as bare names)? Out of scope here — we don't have URLs for those in the JSON.
- Should `models_root()` honor a vibecomfy-specific env var like `VIBECOMFY_MODELS_ROOT` in addition to `COMFY_MODELS_ROOT`? Yes — fall through both before the hardcoded default.

      "description": "Create `vibecomfy/fetch.py` with the downloader. (a) `models_root() -> Path`: fall through `os.environ.get('VIBECOMFY_MODELS_ROOT')` \u2192 `os.environ.get('COMFY_MODELS_ROOT')` \u2192 try `from comfy.cmd.folder_paths import folder_names_and_paths; folder_names_and_paths['checkpoints'][0][0]`'s parent (best-effort, wrapped in try/except) \u2192 `Path('ComfyUI/models')`. No filesystem touch beyond reading env. (b) `local_path(entry)` \u2192 `models_root() / entry['subdir'] / entry['name']`. (c) `is_present(entry)` \u2192 `path.is_file() and path.stat().st_size > 0`. (d) `download(entry, *, force=False, client=None) -> Path`: skip-if-present prints `'skipped {name}'`; build headers, add `Authorization: Bearer {HF_TOKEN}` only if env set; strip `?download=true` from URL; use `client.stream('GET', url, ...)` when `client` is supplied (FLAG-003: do NOT call `httpx.stream` when a client is passed) else `httpx.stream('GET', url, follow_redirects=True, headers=headers, timeout=httpx.Timeout(60, read=None))`; on 401/403 raise `PermissionError(f'License-gated download blocked for {url} \u2014 set HF_TOKEN or accept the license at the source URL.')`; on 404 raise `FileNotFoundError(f'Asset not found at {url}')`; other non-2xx \u2192 `RuntimeError(f'HTTP {status} fetching {url}')`. Stream into `path.with_suffix(path.suffix + '.tmp')`, mkdir parent, then `os.replace(tmp, path)`. Wrap streaming in try/except (including KeyboardInterrupt) that calls `tmp.unlink(missing_ok=True)` before re-raising, so no `.tmp` is left behind. (e) `download_many(entries, *, force=False) -> list[Path]`: sequential; per-entry print one line (`skipped <name>` / `downloaded <name> -> <path>` / `failed <name>: <error>`); continue past failures; raise an aggregate `RuntimeError('N failures')` only at the end so partial progress is preserved. NO new pyproject dep \u2014 httpx already declared. Add `tests/test_fetch.py` covering: `monkeypatch.setenv('VIBECOMFY_MODELS_ROOT', tmp_path)`; skip-when-present; atomic rename (stub `httpx.stream` with a contextmanager returning a fake response with `iter_bytes` and `status_code=200`, assert no `.tmp` remains and final path has expected bytes); 401\u2192PermissionError and 404\u2192FileNotFoundError messages include URL; HF_TOKEN env triggers `Authorization` header (and absence omits it); `?download=true` is stripped from the requested URL; `download_many` continues past failure and raises aggregate `RuntimeError`.",

(flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: vibecomfy/fetch.py\n+from __future__ import annotations\n+\n+import os\n+from pathlib import Path\n+from typing import Any, Mapping\n+from urllib.parse import parse_qsl, urlencode, urlsplit, urlunsplit\n+\n+import httpx\n+\n+\n+def models_root() -> Path:\n+    for env_name in (\"VIBECOMFY_MODELS_ROOT\", \"COMFY_MODELS_ROOT\"):\n+        value = os.environ.get(env_name)\n+        if value:\n+            return Path(value)\n+    try:\n+        from comfy.cmd.folder_paths import folder_names_and_paths\n+\n+        return Path(folder_names_and_paths[\"checkpoints\"][0][0]).parent\n+    except Exception:\n+        return Path(\"ComfyUI/models\")\n+\n+\n+def local_path(entry: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Path:\n+    return models_root() / str(entry[\"subdir\"]) / str(entry[\"name\"])\n+\n+\n+def is_present(entry: Mapping[str, Any]) -> bool:\n+    path = local_path(entry)\n+    return path.is_file() and path.stat().st_size > 0\n+\n+\n+def download(entry: Mapping[str, Any], *, force: bool = False, client: Any = None) -> Path:\n+    path = local_path(entry)\n+    name = str(entry[\"name\"])\n+    if is_present(entry) and not force:\n+        print(f\"skipped {name}\")\n+        return path\n+\n+    url = _strip_download_true(str(entry[\"url\"]))\n+    headers: dict[str, str] = {}\n+    token = os.environ.get(\"HF_TOKEN\")\n+    if token:\n+        headers[\"Authorization\"] = f\"Bearer {token}\"\n+    timeout = httpx.Timeout(60, read=None)\n+    stream_context = (\n+        client.stream(\"GET\", url, follow_redirects=True, headers=headers, timeout=timeout)\n+        if client is not None\n+        else httpx.stream(\"GET\", url, follow_redirects=True, headers=headers, timeout=timeout)\n+    )\n+\n+    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n+    tmp = path.with_suffix(path.suffix + \".tmp\")\n+    try:\n+        with stream_context as response:\n+            _raise_for_status(response.status_code, url)\n+            with tmp.open(\"wb\") as handle:\n+                for chunk in response.iter_bytes():\n+                    if chunk:\n+                        handle.write(chunk)\n+        os.replace(tmp, path)\n+    except BaseException:\n+        tmp.unlink(missing_ok=True)\n+        raise\n+    return path\n+\n+\n+def download_many(entries: list[dict], *, force: bool = False) -> list[Path]:\n+    paths: list[Path] = []\n+    failures = 0\n+    for entry in entries:\n+        name = str(entry.get(\"name\", \"<unknown>\"))\n+        was_present = is_present(entry) and not force\n+        try:\n+            path = download(entry, force=force)\n+        except Exception as exc:\n+            failures += 1\n+            print(f\"failed {name}: {exc}\")\n+            continue\n+        paths.append(path)\n+        if not was_present:\n+            print(f\"downloaded {name} -> {path}\")\n+    if failures:\n+        raise RuntimeError(f\"{failures} failures\")\n+    return paths\n+\n+\n+def _raise_for_status(status_code: int, url: str) -> None:\n+    if status_code in {401, 403}:\n+        raise PermissionError(f\"License-gated download blocked for {url} — set HF_TOKEN or accept the license at the source URL.\")\n+    if status_code == 404:\n+        raise FileNotFoundError(f\"Asset not found at {url}\")\n+    if not 200 <= status_code < 300:\n+        raise RuntimeError(f\"HTTP {status_code} fetching {url}\")\n+\n+\n+def _strip_download_true(url: str) -> str:\n+    parsed = urlsplit(url)\n+    params = [(key, value) for key, value in parse_qsl(parsed.query, keep_blank_values=True) if not (key == \"download\" and value == \"true\")]\n+    return urlunsplit((parsed.scheme, parsed.netloc, parsed.path, parsed.query and urlencode(params), parsed.fragment))\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\"download\", \"download_many\", \"is_present\", \"local_path\", \"models_root\"]\n*** End Patch\n"
}

b\"ok\"\n+    assert (tmp_path / \"checkpoints\" / \"second.safetensors\").read_bytes() == b\"ok\"\n*** End Patch\n"

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy

        Idea:
vibecomfy: model-distribution v1 — extract HuggingFace URLs from workflow JSONs, populate READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"], add a small fetch CLI and doctor integration. Don't build a bundle/manifest format yet.

Source repo: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy

# Problem
Today vibecomfy has a half-built model-distribution story:
- Upstream Comfy-Org workflow JSONs in `workflow_corpus/official/*.json` already carry inline `"url": "https://huggingface.co/..."` fields per model-loader node (see workflow_corpus/official/video/wan_t2v.json:38, ltx2_3_i2v.json:699, flux2_klein_4b_t2i.json:1082, etc.) plus markdown notes in human-readable form.
- The materializer in `scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py` builds `ready_templates/*/*.py` from those JSONs but never extracts the URLs — every scratchpad has `READY_REQUIREMENTS = {"models": [], "custom_nodes": [...]}` with `models` empty.
- There is no downloader. `vibecomfy doctor` checks runtime readiness but doesn't check model presence.
- The de-facto convention is HuggingFace via the upstream Comfy-Org URL fields, but nothing in vibecomfy reads or enforces it.

# Goal
Close the loop in five mechanical steps:
1. Materializer extracts URLs from workflow JSON and populates `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]` with structured entries.
2. A small `vibecomfy.fetch` module downloads missing models from URLs to the right ComfyUI subdir.
3. `vibecomfy fetch <workflow>` CLI command resolves a workflow's model list and downloads the missing ones.
4. `vibecomfy doctor <workflow>` reports missing models with their URLs.
5. Tests for extraction, fetch (mocked httpx), and doctor.

The bundle/manifest format and SHA pinning are explicitly OUT of scope — they're a separate plan.

# Verified facts (do not re-research)
- Inline URL convention is in upstream JSONs:
  - workflow_corpus/official/video/wan_t2v.json:38 — text_encoders/umt5_xxl_fp8_e4m3fn_scaled.safetensors
  - workflow_corpus/official/video/wan_t2v.json:79 — vae/wan_2.1_vae.safetensors
  - workflow_corpus/official/video/wan_t2v.json:200 — diffusion_models/wan2.1_t2v_1.3B_fp16.safetensors
  - The URL field lives at the same JSON level as `class_type` and `inputs` for loader nodes.
- Materializer entry: `scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py` (writes `READY_REQUIREMENTS = {...}` literal into each generated scratchpad).
- Scratchpad shape: `ready_templates/{image,video,edit}/<name>.py` declares `API_WORKFLOW`, `READY_METADATA`, `READY_REQUIREMENTS`, and `build()`.
- Existing CLI registration: `vibecomfy/cli.py` + per-command modules under `vibecomfy/commands/` (e.g. `commands/doctor.py`).
- Existing dep is `httpx>=0.25` (pyproject.toml). No `huggingface_hub` dep — keep it that way for v1.

# Steps

## S1: Extract URLs during materialization
- In `scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py`, walk the source JSON and collect `(node_id, url)` entries from any node that has a top-level `"url"` field.
- For each URL, parse it to derive:
  - `name`: the basename (e.g. `wan2.1_t2v_1.3B_fp16.safetensors`)
  - `subdir`: the ComfyUI models subdir to land it in. Derive from the URL path — for HF Comfy-Org `split_files/<subdir>/<name>` URLs, use `<subdir>` (text_encoders, diffusion_models, vae, clip_vision, loras, latent_upscale_models, etc.). For other URL shapes, fall back to a heuristic based on the loader's `class_type` (CheckpointLoaderSimple → `checkpoints`, CLIPLoader → `text_encoders`, VAELoader → `vae`, LoraLoader → `loras`, ControlNetLoader → `controlnet`, UNETLoader → `diffusion_models`).
  - `url`: the URL as-is (drop `?download=true` query param if present).
- Emit `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]` as a list of `{"name": ..., "url": ..., "subdir": ...}` dicts in materialization order, deduplicated by `(name, subdir)`.
- Re-materialize all existing scratchpads as part of this step.

## S2: vibecomfy.fetch module
- New file `vibecomfy/fetch.py`:
  - `models_root() -> Path` — discover ComfyUI's models root via `comfy.cmd.folder_paths.folder_names_and_paths` if importable, else fall back to `os.environ["COMFY_MODELS_ROOT"]`, else `Path("ComfyUI/models")`.
  - `local_path(entry: dict) -> Path` — `models_root() / entry["subdir"] / entry["name"]`.
  - `is_present(entry: dict) -> bool` — checks the local path exists and has nonzero size.
  - `download(entry: dict, *, force: bool = False) -> Path` — uses `httpx.stream("GET", url, follow_redirects=True)` to download to a `.tmp` sibling file, then rename atomically. Raises `RuntimeError` with a clear message on 401/403 (license gating) and on 404. Skips if `is_present(entry)` and not `force`.
  - `download_many(entries: list[dict], *, force: bool = False) -> list[Path]` — sequential for v1 (parallel is a follow-up). Reports per-entry status via stdout (one line per entry: skipped/downloaded/failed).
- No `huggingface_hub` dep. Use plain HTTP + `Authorization: Bearer $HF_TOKEN` if the env var is set (for license-gated repos).

## S3: vibecomfy fetch CLI command
- New file `vibecomfy/commands/fetch.py`:
  - `vibecomfy fetch <workflow>` — resolves the workflow (same resolution as `inspect`/`doctor`), reads `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]`, calls `download_many`, reports counts.
  - Flags: `--force` (re-download present), `--dry-run` (print what would be fetched).
  - Register in `vibecomfy/cli.py` alongside the other commands.

## S4: doctor integration
- Extend `vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py` to:
  - Read `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]` from the resolved workflow.
  - For each entry, call `is_present`. Report missing entries with name + url + expected local path.
  - Exit nonzero (or surface a flag in the existing doctor output schema) when models are missing.

## S5: Tests
- `tests/test_materialize_extraction.py` — unit-test the URL/subdir derivation against a small fixture JSON (built inline, not snapshot) plus one real upstream sample.
- `tests/test_fetch.py` — mock httpx to verify: skip-when-present, download-to-tmp-then-rename, 401/404 error messages, dry-run.
- `tests/test_doctor_models.py` — verify doctor reports missing models from a fake `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]` and a tmp models root.
- `tests/test_fetch_cli.py` — argparse + dispatch.

# Out of scope (do NOT add)
- Bundle/manifest format (.toml manifest, vibe pack zip, install command). Separate plan.
- SHA256 verification. HF doesn't reliably expose hashes via resolve URLs; first-run hash + cache is a follow-up.
- Civitai-specific auth / API key handling.
- Parallel/multi-connection downloads.
- Auto-fetch on `vibecomfy run` (must be explicit `vibecomfy fetch`).
- A registry / hub for sharing.
- huggingface_hub dependency.

# Design constraints
- Default to HF, but the downloader is URL-driven, not provider-driven. A Civitai/R2/S3 URL works too — only the subdir derivation has an HF-aware fast path.
- Strip `?download=true` query params (Comfy-Org sometimes appends these).
- Strip `split_files/` prefix when deriving subdir from HF Comfy-Org URLs.
- License-gated repos (FLUX.2-klein-base-9b is one): download() returns a clear error pointing at the URL when 401/403, doesn't try to handle the agreement flow.
- Atomic writes (rename from .tmp). No partial files left behind on Ctrl-C.
- No new top-level deps. httpx is already there.
- Sequential downloads; this is v1 not perf-critical.

# Open questions
- Should `vibecomfy fetch` accept a `--workflow` group flag for multi-workflow batch fetching, or stick to one workflow per invocation? Lean toward one per invocation; users can `xargs` if they want.
- Should the materializer also extract custom-node references (already in `READY_REQUIREMENTS["custom_nodes"]` but as bare names)? Out of scope here — we don't have URLs for those in the JSON.
- Should `models_root()` honor a vibecomfy-specific env var like `VIBECOMFY_MODELS_ROOT` in addition to `COMFY_MODELS_ROOT`? Yes — fall through both before the hardcoded default.

    "id": "T4",
    "description": "Create `vibecomfy/fetch.py` with the downloader. (a) `models_root() -> Path`: fall through `os.environ.get('VIBECOMFY_MODELS_ROOT')` \u2192 `os.environ.get('COMFY_MODELS_ROOT')` \u2192 try `from comfy.cmd.folder_paths import folder_names_and_paths; folder_names_and_paths['checkpoints'][0][0]`'s parent (best-effort, wrapped in try/except) \u2192 `Path('ComfyUI/models')`. No filesystem touch beyond reading env. (b) `local_path(entry)` \u2192 `models_root() / entry['subdir'] / entry['name']`. (c) `is_present(entry)` \u2192 `path.is_file() and path.stat().st_size > 0`. (d) `download(entry, *, force=False, client=None) -> Path`: skip-if-present prints `'skipped {name}'`; build headers, add `Authorization: Bearer {HF_TOKEN}` only if env set; strip `?download=true` from URL; use `client.stream('GET', url, ...)` when `client` is supplied (FLAG-003: do NOT call `httpx.stream` when a client is passed) else `httpx.stream('GET', url, follow_redirects=True, headers=headers, timeout=httpx.Timeout(60, read=None))`; on 401/403 raise `PermissionError(f'License-gated download blocked for {url} \u2014 set HF_TOKEN or accept the license at the source URL.')`; on 404 raise `FileNotFoundError(f'Asset not found at {url}')`; other non-2xx \u2192 `RuntimeError(f'HTTP {status} fetching {url}')`. Stream into `path.with_suffix(path.suffix + '.tmp')`, mkdir parent, then `os.replace(tmp, path)`. Wrap streaming in try/except (including KeyboardInterrupt) that calls `tmp.unlink(missing_ok=True)` before re-raising, so no `.tmp` is left behind. (e) `download_many(entries, *, force=False) -> list[Path]`: sequential; per-entry print one line (`skipped <name>` / `downloaded <name> -> <path>` / `failed <name>: <error>`); continue past failures; raise an aggregate `RuntimeError('N failures')` only at the end so partial progress is preserved. NO new pyproject dep \u2014 httpx already declared. Add `tests/test_fetch.py` covering: `monkeypatch.setenv('VIBECOMFY_MODELS_ROOT', tmp_path)`; skip-when-present; atomic rename (stub `httpx.stream` with a contextmanager returning a fake response with `iter_bytes` and `status_code=200`, assert no `.tmp` remains and final path has expected bytes); 401\u2192PermissionError and 404\u2192FileNotFoundError messages include URL; HF_TOKEN env triggers `Authorization` header (and absence omits it); `?download=true` is stripped from the requested URL; `download_many` continues past failure and raises aggregate `RuntimeError`.",
    "depends_on": [
      "T3"
    ],
    "status": "done",
    "executor_notes": "Created vibecomfy/fetch.py with models_root, local_path, is_present, download, and download_many. models_root follows VIBECOMFY_MODELS_ROOT -> COMFY_MODELS_ROOT -> best-effort comfy.cmd.folder_paths checkpoint parent -> ComfyUI/models. download strips ?download=true, adds Authorization only when HF_TOKEN is set, uses a supplied client.stream branch when client is passed and httpx.stream otherwise, maps 401/403 to PermissionError, 404 to FileNotFoundError, other non-2xx to RuntimeError, writes to .tmp then os.replace, and unlinks .tmp on BaseException. download_many is sequential, prints per-entry skipped/downloaded/failed status, continues after failures, and raises aggregate RuntimeError after partial progress. Added tests/test_fetch.py covering env root precedence, skip-when-present, atomic write and tmp cleanup after success/failure, 401/403/404 messages with stripped URLs, HF_TOKEN header presence/absence, ?download=true stripping, supplied-client routing without touching httpx.stream, and aggregate download_many failure behavior. Verification: PYTHONPATH=. pytest tests/test_fetch.py passed (10 passed); PYTHONPATH=. pytest passed (135 passed, 1 skipped).",
