      "description": "Add `vibecomfy fetch <workflow>` CLI command. (a) Create `vibecomfy/commands/fetch.py` with positional `workflow`, flags `--force`, `--dry-run`. Resolve via `load_workflow_reference(args.workflow, schema_provider=get_schema_provider('auto'), allow_scratchpad=True)`. To get structured entries: first try `workflow.metadata.get('model_assets', [])` (populated for scratchpad-loaded workflows by T2's `_merge_requirements`); if empty AND the resolved path is a `.json`, fall back to `extract_from_raw_workflow(load_template(path))`. On `--dry-run`: print one line per entry (`would fetch <name> -> <local_path>` or `present <name>`) and return 0 \u2014 MUST NOT call `download_many`. Otherwise call `download_many(entries, force=args.force)`; return 0 on success, 1 if `download_many` raised the aggregate failure.   "meta_commentary": "Execution order is tight: T2 (`_merge_requirements` tolerance) MUST land before T3 re-materialises any scratchpad, because a freshly-materialised scratchpad with `list[dict]` models would crash the existing dict-sort path on import. Land T2 first even though it conceptually belongs with the materializer changes.\n\nFLAG-003 is the most important open concern \u2014 the plan body literally says `httpx.stream('GET', ...)` but also passes `client: httpx.Client | None`. Implement it as a real branch: `if client is not None: stream_ctx = client.stream('GET', url, follow_redirects=True, headers=headers, timeout=...)` else `stream_ctx = httpx.stream(...)`. Without this, the test injection point is dead and the mocked-streaming tests will be brittle/awkward.\n\nThe FLUX2 GGUF gap is intentional. Don't try to resolve it. The plan's policy is: leave it unresolved in v1, document inline in `_apply_flux2_gguf_policy`, surface the gap via the smoke test. A follow-up plan will populate `model_assets_extra` with the chosen URL once a canonical source is picked.\n\nFor the post-policy filter (T3), the FLUX2 case is the canonical test: the source JSON advertises `flux-2-klein-base-9b-fp8.safetensors`, `qwen_3_8b_fp8mixed.safetensors`, `full_encoder_small_decoder.safetensors`, but the policy renames the diffusion UNET to `flux-2-klein-9b-Q4_K_M.gguf` and the VAE to `flux2-vae.safetensors`. After the filter, only the qwen text encoder should survive (its filename is unchanged by policy). Verify by reading the regenerated `ready_templates/image/flux2_klein_9b_gguf_t2i.py`.\n\nThe walk order in T1 matters for determinism. Sort top-level nodes by `int(node['id'])` (handle string ids that look like ints), then visit subgraphs in definition order, then within each subgraph also sort by `int(node['id'])`. Subgraph node IDs are NOT globally unique across nesting levels \u2014 the dedup key is `(name, subdir)`, not node id.\n\nDon't add `huggingface_hub`, `tqdm`, or any progress library. httpx + plain prints is the v1 contract.\n\nDon't auto-fetch on `vibecomfy run`. The CLI command is explicit.\n\nWatch for incidental regressions in `tests/test_ready_templates.py` after re-materialisation. The plan claims `test_ready_template_preserves_materialized_requirements` only asserts custom_nodes \u2014 verify that assumption when the test runs (T7 step 3); if it actually inspects `models`, you may need to update it to accept the new shape.\n\nDon't create a new test file for the throwaway repro in T7 \u2014 write it to `/tmp/repro_<thing>.py`, run it, then delete it. The user's instructions explicitly say to delete it after confirming the fix.\n\nFor the `models_root()` `comfy.cmd.folder_paths` probe in T4 \u2014 wrap the entire import-and-lookup in `try: ... except Exception:` so that absence of comfy or any AttributeError silently falls through to the next layer. Don't let an import error leak to callers.",

- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the path-mapping defect from the previous iteration is fixed, but a different technical gap remains around `self_refiner`. repository search shows `shared.utils.self_refiner` is imported by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/distilled.py`, `ti2vid_one_stage.py`, `ti2vid_two_stages.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/helpers.py`, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`; the v4 plan still aligns `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` to upstream head without any concrete runtime step that exercises those code paths after the change, so the only repo-documented behavior check for that file is still missing from the executable body. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-success-criteria-well-prioritized-and-verifiable: are the success criteria well-prioritized and verifiable?: the attached success criteria are stale and do not match the current plan body. they still require 'no `usestate` for `activepairdata`', '`setactivepairdata` fully removed', 'sync effect deleted', and '`onpairclick` simplified to `(pairindex: number) => void`', which are the opposite of the current targeted-sync plan. as presented, the criteria are not usable for review or execution. > DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy

        Idea:
vibecomfy: model-distribution v1 — extract HuggingFace URLs from workflow JSONs, populate READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"], add a small fetch CLI and doctor integration. Don't build a bundle/manifest format yet.

Source repo: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy

# Problem
Today vibecomfy has a half-built model-distribution story:
- Upstream Comfy-Org workflow JSONs in `workflow_corpus/official/*.json` already carry inline `"url": "https://huggingface.co/..."` fields per model-loader node (see workflow_corpus/official/video/wan_t2v.json:38, ltx2_3_i2v.json:699, flux2_klein_4b_t2i.json:1082, etc.) plus markdown notes in human-readable form.
- The materializer in `scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py` builds `ready_templates/*/*.py` from those JSONs but never extracts the URLs — every scratchpad has `READY_REQUIREMENTS = {"models": [], "custom_nodes": [...]}` with `models` empty.
- There is no downloader. `vibecomfy doctor` checks runtime readiness but doesn't check model presence.
- The de-facto convention is HuggingFace via the upstream Comfy-Org URL fields, but nothing in vibecomfy reads or enforces it.

# Goal
Close the loop in five mechanical steps:
1. Materializer extracts URLs from workflow JSON and populates `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]` with structured entries.
2. A small `vibecomfy.fetch` module downloads missing models from URLs to the right ComfyUI subdir.
3. `vibecomfy fetch <workflow>` CLI command resolves a workflow's model list and downloads the missing ones.
4. `vibecomfy doctor <workflow>` reports missing models with their URLs.
5. Tests for extraction, fetch (mocked httpx), and doctor.

The bundle/manifest format and SHA pinning are explicitly OUT of scope — they're a separate plan.

# Verified facts (do not re-research)
- Inline URL convention is in upstream JSONs:
  - workflow_corpus/official/video/wan_t2v.json:38 — text_encoders/umt5_xxl_fp8_e4m3fn_scaled.safetensors
  - workflow_corpus/official/video/wan_t2v.json:79 — vae/wan_2.1_vae.safetensors
  - workflow_corpus/official/video/wan_t2v.json:200 — diffusion_models/wan2.1_t2v_1.3B_fp16.safetensors
  - The URL field lives at the same JSON level as `class_type` and `inputs` for loader nodes.
- Materializer entry: `scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py` (writes `READY_REQUIREMENTS = {...}` literal into each generated scratchpad).
- Scratchpad shape: `ready_templates/{image,video,edit}/<name>.py` declares `API_WORKFLOW`, `READY_METADATA`, `READY_REQUIREMENTS`, and `build()`.
- Existing CLI registration: `vibecomfy/cli.py` + per-command modules under `vibecomfy/commands/` (e.g. `commands/doctor.py`).
- Existing dep is `httpx>=0.25` (pyproject.toml). No `huggingface_hub` dep — keep it that way for v1.

# Steps

## S1: Extract URLs during materialization
- In `scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py`, walk the source JSON and collect `(node_id, url)` entries from any node that has a top-level `"url"` field.
- For each URL, parse it to derive:
  - `name`: the basename (e.g. `wan2.1_t2v_1.3B_fp16.safetensors`)
  - `subdir`: the ComfyUI models subdir to land it in. Derive from the URL path — for HF Comfy-Org `split_files/<subdir>/<name>` URLs, use `<subdir>` (text_encoders, diffusion_models, vae, clip_vision, loras, latent_upscale_models, etc.). For other URL shapes, fall back to a heuristic based on the loader's `class_type` (CheckpointLoaderSimple → `checkpoints`, CLIPLoader → `text_encoders`, VAELoader → `vae`, LoraLoader → `loras`, ControlNetLoader → `controlnet`, UNETLoader → `diffusion_models`).
  - `url`: the URL as-is (drop `?download=true` query param if present).
- Emit `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]` as a list of `{"name": ..., "url": ..., "subdir": ...}` dicts in materialization order, deduplicated by `(name, subdir)`.
- Re-materialize all existing scratchpads as part of this step.

## S2: vibecomfy.fetch module
- New file `vibecomfy/fetch.py`:
  - `models_root() -> Path` — discover ComfyUI's models root via `comfy.cmd.folder_paths.folder_names_and_paths` if importable, else fall back to `os.environ["COMFY_MODELS_ROOT"]`, else `Path("ComfyUI/models")`.
  - `local_path(entry: dict) -> Path` — `models_root() / entry["subdir"] / entry["name"]`.
  - `is_present(entry: dict) -> bool` — checks the local path exists and has nonzero size.
  - `download(entry: dict, *, force: bool = False) -> Path` — uses `httpx.stream("GET", url, follow_redirects=True)` to download to a `.tmp` sibling file, then rename atomically. Raises `RuntimeError` with a clear message on 401/403 (license gating) and on 404. Skips if `is_present(entry)` and not `force`.
  - `download_many(entries: list[dict], *, force: bool = False) -> list[Path]` — sequential for v1 (parallel is a follow-up). Reports per-entry status via stdout (one line per entry: skipped/downloaded/failed).
- No `huggingface_hub` dep. Use plain HTTP + `Authorization: Bearer $HF_TOKEN` if the env var is set (for license-gated repos).

## S3: vibecomfy fetch CLI command
- New file `vibecomfy/commands/fetch.py`:
  - `vibecomfy fetch <workflow>` — resolves the workflow (same resolution as `inspect`/`doctor`), reads `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]`, calls `download_many`, reports counts.
  - Flags: `--force` (re-download present), `--dry-run` (print what would be fetched).
  - Register in `vibecomfy/cli.py` alongside the other commands.

## S4: doctor integration
- Extend `vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py` to:
  - Read `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]` from the resolved workflow.
  - For each entry, call `is_present`. Report missing entries with name + url + expected local path.
  - Exit nonzero (or surface a flag in the existing doctor output schema) when models are missing.

## S5: Tests
- `tests/test_materialize_extraction.py` — unit-test the URL/subdir derivation against a small fixture JSON (built inline, not snapshot) plus one real upstream sample.
- `tests/test_fetch.py` — mock httpx to verify: skip-when-present, download-to-tmp-then-rename, 401/404 error messages, dry-run.
- `tests/test_doctor_models.py` — verify doctor reports missing models from a fake `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]` and a tmp models root.
- `tests/test_fetch_cli.py` — argparse + dispatch.

# Out of scope (do NOT add)
- Bundle/manifest format (.toml manifest, vibe pack zip, install command). Separate plan.
- SHA256 verification. HF doesn't reliably expose hashes via resolve URLs; first-run hash + cache is a follow-up.
- Civitai-specific auth / API key handling.
- Parallel/multi-connection downloads.
- Auto-fetch on `vibecomfy run` (must be explicit `vibecomfy fetch`).
- A registry / hub for sharing.
- huggingface_hub dependency.

# Design constraints
- Default to HF, but the downloader is URL-driven, not provider-driven. A Civitai/R2/S3 URL works too — only the subdir derivation has an HF-aware fast path.
- Strip `?download=true` query params (Comfy-Org sometimes appends these).
- Strip `split_files/` prefix when deriving subdir from HF Comfy-Org URLs.
- License-gated repos (FLUX.2-klein-base-9b is one): download() returns a clear error pointing at the URL when 401/403, doesn't try to handle the agreement flow.
- Atomic writes (rename from .tmp). No partial files left behind on Ctrl-C.
- No new top-level deps. httpx is already there.
- Sequential downloads; this is v1 not perf-critical.

# Open questions
- Should `vibecomfy fetch` accept a `--workflow` group flag for multi-workflow batch fetching, or stick to one workflow per invocation? Lean toward one per invocation; users can `xargs` if they want.
- Should the materializer also extract custom-node references (already in `READY_REQUIREMENTS["custom_nodes"]` but as bare names)? Out of scope here — we don't have URLs for those in the JSON.
- Should `models_root()` honor a vibecomfy-specific env var like `VIBECOMFY_MODELS_ROOT` in addition to `COMFY_MODELS_ROOT`? Yes — fall through both before the hardcoded default.

Add `_missing_model_warnings(workflow, path)`: get entries the same way fetch does (`workflow.metadata['model_assets']`, fall back to `extract_from_raw_workflow(load_template(path))` if path is `.json`); for each entry call `vibecomfy.fetch.is_present(entry)`; for misses append `f'missing model {entry[\"name\"]}: expected {local_path(entry)} \u2014 fetch from {entry[\"url\"]}'`. (a) `models_root() -> Path`: fall through `os.environ.get('VIBECOMFY_MODELS_ROOT')` \u2192 `os.environ.get('COMFY_MODELS_ROOT')` \u2192 try `from comfy.cmd.folder_paths import folder_names_and_paths; folder_names_and_paths['checkpoints'][0][0]`'s parent (best-effort, wrapped in try/except) \u2192 `Path('ComfyUI/models')`. No filesystem touch beyond reading env. (b) `local_path(entry)` \u2192 `models_root() / entry['subdir'] / entry['name']`. (c) `is_present(entry)` \u2192 `path.is_file() and path.stat().st_size > 0`. (d) `download(entry, *, force=False, client=None) -> Path`: skip-if-present prints `'skipped {name}'`; build headers, add `Authorization: Bearer {HF_TOKEN}` only if env set; strip `?download=true` from URL; use `client.stream('GET', url, ...)` when `client` is supplied (FLAG-003: do NOT call `httpx.stream` when a client is passed) else `httpx.stream('GET', url, follow_redirects=True, headers=headers, timeout=httpx.Timeout(60, read=None))`; on 401/403 raise `PermissionError(f'License-gated download blocked for {url} \u2014 set HF_TOKEN or accept the license at the source URL.')`; on 404 raise `FileNotFoundError(f'Asset not found at {url}')`; other non-2xx \u2192 `RuntimeError(f'HTTP {status} fetching {url}')`. Stream into `path.with_suffix(path.suffix + '.tmp')`, mkdir parent, then `os.replace(tmp, path)`. Wrap streaming in try/except (including KeyboardInterrupt) that calls `tmp.unlink(missing_ok=True)` before re-raising, so no `.tmp` is left behind. (e) `download_many(entries, *, force=False) -> list[Path]`: sequential; per-entry print one line (`skipped <name>` / `downloaded <name> -> <path>` / `failed <name>: <error>`); continue past failures; raise an aggregate `RuntimeError('N failures')` only at the end so partial progress is preserved. NO new pyproject dep \u2014 httpx already declared. Add `tests/test_fetch.py` covering: `monkeypatch.setenv('VIBECOMFY_MODELS_ROOT', tmp_path)`; skip-when-present; atomic rename (stub `httpx.stream` with a contextmanager returning a fake response with `iter_bytes` and `status_code=200`, assert no `.tmp` remains and final path has expected bytes); 401\u2192PermissionError and 404\u2192FileNotFoundError messages include URL; HF_TOKEN env triggers `Authorization` header (and absence omits it); `?download=true` is stripped from the requested URL; `download_many` continues past failure and raises aggregate `RuntimeError`.",

      "id": "T4",
      "description": "Create `vibecomfy/fetch.py` with the downloader. (a) `models_root() -> Path`: fall through `os.environ.get('VIBECOMFY_MODELS_ROOT')` \u2192 `os.environ.get('COMFY_MODELS_ROOT')` \u2192 try `from comfy.cmd.folder_paths import folder_names_and_paths; folder_names_and_paths['checkpoints'][0][0]`'s parent (best-effort, wrapped in try/except) \u2192 `Path('ComfyUI/models')`. No filesystem touch beyond reading env. (b) `local_path(entry)` \u2192 `models_root() / entry['subdir'] / entry['name']`. (c) `is_present(entry)` \u2192 `path.is_file() and path.stat().st_size > 0`. (d) `download(entry, *, force=False, client=None) -> Path`: skip-if-present prints `'skipped {name}'`; build headers, add `Authorization: Bearer {HF_TOKEN}` only if env set; strip `?download=true` from URL; use `client.stream('GET', url, ...)` when `client` is supplied (FLAG-003: do NOT call `httpx.stream` when a client is passed) else `httpx.stream('GET', url, follow_redirects=True, headers=headers, timeout=httpx.Timeout(60, read=None))`; on 401/403 raise `PermissionError(f'License-gated download blocked for {url} \u2014 set HF_TOKEN or accept the license at the source URL.')`; on 404 raise `FileNotFoundError(f'Asset not found at {url}')`; other non-2xx \u2192 `RuntimeError(f'HTTP {status} fetching {url}')`. Stream into `path.with_suffix(path.suffix + '.tmp')`, mkdir parent, then `os.replace(tmp, path)`. Wrap streaming in try/except (including KeyboardInterrupt) that calls `tmp.unlink(missing_ok=True)` before re-raising, so no `.tmp` is left behind. (e) `download_many(entries, *, force=False) -> list[Path]`: sequential; per-entry print one line (`skipped <name>` / `downloaded <name> -> <path>` / `failed <name>: <error>`); continue past failures; raise an aggregate `RuntimeError('N failures')` only at the end so partial progress is preserved. NO new pyproject dep \u2014 httpx already declared. Add `tests/test_fetch.py` covering: `monkeypatch.setenv('VIBECOMFY_MODELS_ROOT', tmp_path)`; skip-when-present; atomic rename (stub `httpx.stream` with a contextmanager returning a fake response with `iter_bytes` and `status_code=200`, assert no `.tmp` remains and final path has expected bytes); 401\u2192PermissionError and 404\u2192FileNotFoundError messages include URL; HF_TOKEN env triggers `Authorization` header (and absence omits it); `?download=true` is stripped from the requested URL; `download_many` continues past failure and raises aggregate `RuntimeError`.",

  "meta_commentary": "Execution order is tight: T2 (`_merge_requirements` tolerance) MUST land before T3 re-materialises any scratchpad, because a freshly-materialised scratchpad with `list[dict]` models would crash the existing dict-sort path on import. Land T2 first even though it conceptually belongs with the materializer changes.\n\nFLAG-003 is the most important open concern \u2014 the plan body literally says `httpx.stream('GET', ...)` but also passes `client: httpx.Client | None`. Implement it as a real branch: `if client is not None: stream_ctx = client.stream('GET', url, follow_redirects=True, headers=headers, timeout=...)` else `stream_ctx = httpx.stream(...)`. Without this, the test injection point is dead and the mocked-streaming tests will be brittle/awkward.\n\nThe FLUX2 GGUF gap is intentional. Don't try to resolve it. The plan's policy is: leave it unresolved in v1, document inline in `_apply_flux2_gguf_policy`, surface the gap via the smoke test. A follow-up plan will populate `model_assets_extra` with the chosen URL once a canonical source is picked.\n\nFor the post-policy filter (T3), the FLUX2 case is the canonical test: the source JSON advertises `flux-2-klein-base-9b-fp8.safetensors`, `qwen_3_8b_fp8mixed.safetensors`, `full_encoder_small_decoder.safetensors`, but the policy renames the diffusion UNET to `flux-2-klein-9b-Q4_K_M.gguf` and the VAE to `flux2-vae.safetensors`. After the filter, only the qwen text encoder should survive (its filename is unchanged by policy). Verify by reading the regenerated `ready_templates/image/flux2_klein_9b_gguf_t2i.py`.\n\nThe walk order in T1 matters for determinism. Sort top-level nodes by `int(node['id'])` (handle string ids that look like ints), then visit subgraphs in definition order, then within each subgraph also sort by `int(node['id'])`. Subgraph node IDs are NOT globally unique across nesting levels \u2014 the dedup key is `(name, subdir)`, not node id.\n\nDon't add `huggingface_hub`, `tqdm`, or any progress library. httpx + plain prints is the v1 contract.\n\nDon't auto-fetch on `vibecomfy run`. The CLI command is explicit.\n\nWatch for incidental regressions in `tests/test_ready_templates.py` after re-materialisation. The plan claims `test_ready_template_preserves_materialized_requirements` only asserts custom_nodes \u2014 verify that assumption when the test runs (T7 step 3); if it actually inspects `models`, you may need to update it to accept the new shape.\n\nDon't create a new test file for the throwaway repro in T7 \u2014 write it to `/tmp/repro_<thing>.py`, run it, then delete it. The user's instructions explicitly say to delete it after confirming the fix.\n\nFor the `models_root()` `comfy.cmd.folder_paths` probe in T4 \u2014 wrap the entire import-and-lookup in `try: ... except Exception:` so that absence of comfy or any AttributeError silently falls through to the next layer. Don't let an import error leak to callers.",

- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.         Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy

        Idea:
vibecomfy: model-distribution v1 — extract HuggingFace URLs from workflow JSONs, populate READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"], add a small fetch CLI and doctor integration. Don't build a bundle/manifest format yet.

Source repo: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy

# Problem
Today vibecomfy has a half-built model-distribution story:
- Upstream Comfy-Org workflow JSONs in `workflow_corpus/official/*.json` already carry inline `"url": "https://huggingface.co/..."` fields per model-loader node (see workflow_corpus/official/video/wan_t2v.json:38, ltx2_3_i2v.json:699, flux2_klein_4b_t2i.json:1082, etc.) plus markdown notes in human-readable form.
- The materializer in `scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py` builds `ready_templates/*/*.py` from those JSONs but never extracts the URLs — every scratchpad has `READY_REQUIREMENTS = {"models": [], "custom_nodes": [...]}` with `models` empty.
- There is no downloader. `vibecomfy doctor` checks runtime readiness but doesn't check model presence.
- The de-facto convention is HuggingFace via the upstream Comfy-Org URL fields, but nothing in vibecomfy reads or enforces it.

# Goal
Close the loop in five mechanical steps:
1. Materializer extracts URLs from workflow JSON and populates `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]` with structured entries.
2. A small `vibecomfy.fetch` module downloads missing models from URLs to the right ComfyUI subdir.
3. `vibecomfy fetch <workflow>` CLI command resolves a workflow's model list and downloads the missing ones.
4. `vibecomfy doctor <workflow>` reports missing models with their URLs.
5. Tests for extraction, fetch (mocked httpx), and doctor.

The bundle/manifest format and SHA pinning are explicitly OUT of scope — they're a separate plan.

# Verified facts (do not re-research)
- Inline URL convention is in upstream JSONs:
  - workflow_corpus/official/video/wan_t2v.json:38 — text_encoders/umt5_xxl_fp8_e4m3fn_scaled.safetensors
  - workflow_corpus/official/video/wan_t2v.json:79 — vae/wan_2.1_vae.safetensors
  - workflow_corpus/official/video/wan_t2v.json:200 — diffusion_models/wan2.1_t2v_1.3B_fp16.safetensors
  - The URL field lives at the same JSON level as `class_type` and `inputs` for loader nodes.
- Materializer entry: `scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py` (writes `READY_REQUIREMENTS = {...}` literal into each generated scratchpad).
- Scratchpad shape: `ready_templates/{image,video,edit}/<name>.py` declares `API_WORKFLOW`, `READY_METADATA`, `READY_REQUIREMENTS`, and `build()`.
- Existing CLI registration: `vibecomfy/cli.py` + per-command modules under `vibecomfy/commands/` (e.g. `commands/doctor.py`).
- Existing dep is `httpx>=0.25` (pyproject.toml). No `huggingface_hub` dep — keep it that way for v1.

# Steps

## S1: Extract URLs during materialization
- In `scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py`, walk the source JSON and collect `(node_id, url)` entries from any node that has a top-level `"url"` field.
- For each URL, parse it to derive:
  - `name`: the basename (e.g. `wan2.1_t2v_1.3B_fp16.safetensors`)
  - `subdir`: the ComfyUI models subdir to land it in. Derive from the URL path — for HF Comfy-Org `split_files/<subdir>/<name>` URLs, use `<subdir>` (text_encoders, diffusion_models, vae, clip_vision, loras, latent_upscale_models, etc.). For other URL shapes, fall back to a heuristic based on the loader's `class_type` (CheckpointLoaderSimple → `checkpoints`, CLIPLoader → `text_encoders`, VAELoader → `vae`, LoraLoader → `loras`, ControlNetLoader → `controlnet`, UNETLoader → `diffusion_models`).
  - `url`: the URL as-is (drop `?download=true` query param if present).
- Emit `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]` as a list of `{"name": ..., "url": ..., "subdir": ...}` dicts in materialization order, deduplicated by `(name, subdir)`.
- Re-materialize all existing scratchpads as part of this step.

## S2: vibecomfy.fetch module
- New file `vibecomfy/fetch.py`:
  - `models_root() -> Path` — discover ComfyUI's models root via `comfy.cmd.folder_paths.folder_names_and_paths` if importable, else fall back to `os.environ["COMFY_MODELS_ROOT"]`, else `Path("ComfyUI/models")`.
  - `local_path(entry: dict) -> Path` — `models_root() / entry["subdir"] / entry["name"]`.
  - `is_present(entry: dict) -> bool` — checks the local path exists and has nonzero size.
  - `download(entry: dict, *, force: bool = False) -> Path` — uses `httpx.stream("GET", url, follow_redirects=True)` to download to a `.tmp` sibling file, then rename atomically. Raises `RuntimeError` with a clear message on 401/403 (license gating) and on 404. Skips if `is_present(entry)` and not `force`.
  - `download_many(entries: list[dict], *, force: bool = False) -> list[Path]` — sequential for v1 (parallel is a follow-up). Reports per-entry status via stdout (one line per entry: skipped/downloaded/failed).
- No `huggingface_hub` dep. Use plain HTTP + `Authorization: Bearer $HF_TOKEN` if the env var is set (for license-gated repos).

## S3: vibecomfy fetch CLI command
- New file `vibecomfy/commands/fetch.py`:
  - `vibecomfy fetch <workflow>` — resolves the workflow (same resolution as `inspect`/`doctor`), reads `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]`, calls `download_many`, reports counts.
  - Flags: `--force` (re-download present), `--dry-run` (print what would be fetched).
  - Register in `vibecomfy/cli.py` alongside the other commands.

## S4: doctor integration
- Extend `vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py` to:
  - Read `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]` from the resolved workflow.
  - For each entry, call `is_present`. Report missing entries with name + url + expected local path.
  - Exit nonzero (or surface a flag in the existing doctor output schema) when models are missing.

## S5: Tests
- `tests/test_materialize_extraction.py` — unit-test the URL/subdir derivation against a small fixture JSON (built inline, not snapshot) plus one real upstream sample.
- `tests/test_fetch.py` — mock httpx to verify: skip-when-present, download-to-tmp-then-rename, 401/404 error messages, dry-run.
- `tests/test_doctor_models.py` — verify doctor reports missing models from a fake `READY_REQUIREMENTS["models"]` and a tmp models root.
- `tests/test_fetch_cli.py` — argparse + dispatch.

# Out of scope (do NOT add)
- Bundle/manifest format (.toml manifest, vibe pack zip, install command). Separate plan.
- SHA256 verification. HF doesn't reliably expose hashes via resolve URLs; first-run hash + cache is a follow-up.
- Civitai-specific auth / API key handling.
- Parallel/multi-connection downloads.
- Auto-fetch on `vibecomfy run` (must be explicit `vibecomfy fetch`).
- A registry / hub for sharing.
- huggingface_hub dependency.

# Design constraints
- Default to HF, but the downloader is URL-driven, not provider-driven. A Civitai/R2/S3 URL works too — only the subdir derivation has an HF-aware fast path.
- Strip `?download=true` query params (Comfy-Org sometimes appends these).
- Strip `split_files/` prefix when deriving subdir from HF Comfy-Org URLs.
- License-gated repos (FLUX.2-klein-base-9b is one): download() returns a clear error pointing at the URL when 401/403, doesn't try to handle the agreement flow.
- Atomic writes (rename from .tmp). No partial files left behind on Ctrl-C.
- No new top-level deps. httpx is already there.
- Sequential downloads; this is v1 not perf-critical.

# Open questions
- Should `vibecomfy fetch` accept a `--workflow` group flag for multi-workflow batch fetching, or stick to one workflow per invocation? Lean toward one per invocation; users can `xargs` if they want.
- Should the materializer also extract custom-node references (already in `READY_REQUIREMENTS["custom_nodes"]` but as bare names)? Out of scope here — we don't have URLs for those in the JSON.
- Should `models_root()` honor a vibecomfy-specific env var like `VIBECOMFY_MODELS_ROOT` in addition to `COMFY_MODELS_ROOT`? Yes — fall through both before the hardcoded default.

(d) `download(entry, *, force=False, client=None) -> Path`: skip-if-present prints `'skipped {name}'`; build headers, add `Authorization: Bearer {HF_TOKEN}` only if env set; strip `?download=true` from URL; use `client.stream('GET', url, ...)` when `client` is supplied (FLAG-003: do NOT call `httpx.stream` when a client is passed) else `httpx.stream('GET', url, follow_redirects=True, headers=headers, timeout=httpx.Timeout(60, read=None))`; on 401/403 raise `PermissionError(f'License-gated download blocked for {url} \u2014 set HF_TOKEN or accept the license at the source URL.')`; on 404 raise `FileNotFoundError(f'Asset not found at {url}')`; other non-2xx \u2192 `RuntimeError(f'HTTP {status} fetching {url}')`. Stream into `path.with_suffix(path.suffix + '.tmp')`, mkdir parent, then `os.replace(tmp, path)`. Wrap streaming in try/except (including KeyboardInterrupt) that calls `tmp.unlink(missing_ok=True)` before re-raising, so no `.tmp` is left behind. (e) `download_many(entries, *, force=False) -> list[Path]`: sequential; per-entry print one line (`skipped <name>` / `downloaded <name> -> <path>` / `failed <name>: <error>`); continue past failures; raise an aggregate `RuntimeError('N failures')` only at the end so partial progress is preserved. NO new pyproject dep \u2014 httpx already declared. Add `tests/test_fetch.py` covering: `monkeypatch.setenv('VIBECOMFY_MODELS_ROOT', tmp_path)`; skip-when-present; atomic rename (stub `httpx.stream` with a contextmanager returning a fake response with `iter_bytes` and `status_code=200`, assert no `.tmp` remains and final path has expected bytes); 401\u2192PermissionError and 404\u2192FileNotFoundError messages include URL; HF_TOKEN env triggers `Authorization` header (and absence omits it); `?download=true` is stripped from the requested URL; `download_many` continues past failure and raises aggregate `RuntimeError`.",

- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
